<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-13D1D0B6-B1C7-4010-8684-F52A0FF010A0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A school has $1120$ pupils, $652$ pupils are boys. How many pupils are girls?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total number of girls}   &= 1120 - 625 \\\\
                                &= 495
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$495$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total number of girls}   &= 1120 - 625 \\\\
                                &= 495
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$495$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5BDF797D-4BC1-47EE-9D46-F0012478D4C3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$\boxed{\phantom{5}} + (5 \times \boxed{\phantom{5}}) = 42$

What number does $\boxed{\phantom{5}}$ stand for?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (5 \times a)    &= 42 \\\\
a + 5a              &= 42 \\\\
6a                  &= 42 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {42} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {7 \times 6} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {6} } {\cancel{6}} \\\\
a                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (5 \times a)    &= 42 \\\\
a + 5a              &= 42 \\\\
6a                  &= 42 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {42} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {7 \times 6} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {6} } {\cancel{6}} \\\\
a                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

A train has $6$ carriages, Each carriage holds $88$ passengers. How many seats has the train have?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question, the number of seats in a carriage = 88,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total seats in the train}  &= 88 \times 6 \\\\
                                 &= 528
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$528$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

$

                                 &= 528
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5DA5AA77-9849-4E05-A7A5-DB8108529848'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Two fifths of the pupils in a school wear glasses. What percentage of the pupils wear glasses?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of pupils with glasses}    &= \dfrac {2} {5} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                                            &= 40 \ \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40 \ \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of pupils with glasses}    &= \dfrac {2} {5} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                                            &= 40 \ \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$40 \ \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B9065A5E-7C52-4C0F-A700-FFA2337EB5BC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A parcel weighs $3.7\ \text{kg}$. What is its weight in $\text{grams}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  3.7 \ \text {kg} \\\\
&=  3.7 \times 1000 \ \text {g} \\\\
&=  3700 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3700 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=  3.7 \ \text {kg} \\\\
&=  3.7 \times 1000 \ \text {g} \\\\
&=  3700 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3700 \ \text {g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F735C697-C1D6-4F28-881B-25D75A4D04A1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The UK population is approximately $66{,}482{,}000$. The population of London is $8.78 \ \text{million}$. How many people in the UK live outside London?


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Population outside London}    &= 66{,}482{,}000 - 8.78 \ \text{million} \\\\
                                    &= 66482000 - 8780000 \\\\
                                    &= 57{,}702{,}000
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$57{,}702{,}000$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Population outside London}    &= 66{,}482{,}000 - 8.78 \ \text{million} \\\\
                                    &= 66482000 - 8780000 \\\\
                                    &= 57{,}702{,}000
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$57{,}702{,}000$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C354CC85-6043-4133-9B50-EDA130A479B3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many times can $147$ be subtracted from $1270$ before we get to zero?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {1270} {147} \\\\
                        &= 8 \dfrac {94} {147} \\\\
                        &= 8
\end{aligned}
$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {1270} {147} \\\\
                        &= 8 \dfrac {94} {147} \\\\
                        &= 8
\end{aligned}
$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

$20 \quad -4 \quad -28 \quad -52, \quad -76$

What is the step between successive numbers in this sequence?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR:  Let's find the relation between the consecutive terms of the sequence.


The next term is calculated by subtracting $24$ from the previous term. Hence, the sequence would be:

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(-24)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(-24)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(-24)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(-24)} }  \\
20,  &&    -4,  &&  -28,  &&   -52,     &&  -76
\end{matrix}
$

Step between successive terms $= 24$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$24$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_sequence_find_difference_note

20,  &&    -4,  &&  -28,  &&   -52,     &&  -76
\end{matrix}
$

Step between successive terms $= 24$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D648BFB2-9E92-4C01-96BE-8EBD7EBD85A3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A square has the same area as the rectangle below. What is the length of its side?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$


Let the side of square $= s$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectangle}      &= 6 \times 24 \\\\
                               &= 144 \ \text {cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of square} &= \text{Area of rectangle} \\\\
s \times s            &= 144 \\\\
s ^ 2                 &= 144 \\\\
s                     &= \sqrt {144} \\\\
s                     &= 12 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length of the side of the square is $12 \ \text {cm}$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12 \ \text {cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_rectangle_expanded

rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_square_expanded

Let the side of square $= s$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectangle}      &= 6 \times 24 \\\\
                               &= 144 \ \text {cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of square} &= \text{Area of rectangle} \\\\
s \times s            &= 144 \\\\
s ^ 2                 &= 144 \\\\
s                     &= \sqrt {144} \\\\
s                     &= 12 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Length of the side of the square is $12 \ \text {cm}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12 \ \text {cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A2C2EC3B-3FB5-49C8-859C-0063205198F7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $\dfrac {3} {4}$ of $\pounds 4.48$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ \pounds 4.48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 4.48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4.48} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13.44} {4} \\\\
&= \pounds 3.36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 3.36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ \pounds 4.48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 4.48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4.48} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13.44} {4} \\\\
&= \pounds 3.36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 3.36$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F1714A09-DE83-40BB-B858-8620CDD58B7E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of these lengths is longest?

$157\ \text{mm} \quad 52\ \text{cm} \quad 0.451\ \text{m} \quad 43\ \text{cm} \quad 0.5 \ \text{m}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

To compare, let's can convert all the units to $\text {cm}$. 

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 157 \ \text{mm}, \quad 52 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.451 \ \text{m}, \quad 43 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.5  \ \text{m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {157} {10} \ \text{cm}, \quad 52 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.451 \times 100 \ \text{cm}, \quad 43 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.5 \times 100 \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 15.7 \ \text{cm}, \quad 52 \ \text{cm}, \quad 45.1 \ \text{cm}, \quad 43 \ \text{cm}, \quad 50  \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

The longest length $= 52 \ \text{cm}$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$52 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>To compare, let's can convert all the units to $\text {cm}$. 

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 157 \ \text{mm}, \quad 52 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.451 \ \text{m}, \quad 43 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.5  \ \text{m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {157} {10} \ \text{cm}, \quad 52 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.451 \times 100 \ \text{cm}, \quad 43 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.5 \times 100 \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 15.7 \ \text{cm}, \quad 52 \ \text{cm}, \quad 45.1 \ \text{cm}, \quad 43 \ \text{cm}, \quad 50  \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

The longest length $= 52 \ \text{cm}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$52 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-82406E83-C27E-418F-BD06-271C72DD1BA8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

To get the next number in the sequence you multiply by three and add one.

$3 \quad 10 \quad 31 \quad 94$

What is the $6 \text{th}$ number in the sequence?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1F8DFCED-55B0-441C-B4D6-0CEFC9911351'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A stationery shop sells $6851$ pens in $31$ days. How many pens per day did they sell?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of pens sold in a day $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 31     &= 6851 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {6851} {31} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {221 \times \cancel {31}} {\cancel {31}} \\\\
a               &= 221
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$221$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of pens sold in a day $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 31     &= 6851 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {6851} {31} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {221 \times \cancel {31}} {\cancel {31}} \\\\
a               &= 221
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$221$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-011C7343-EA2D-4EDF-AFE0-7803C6D34E4D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

If two angles of a triangle are $36 \degree$ and $22 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$


Let the size of the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 36 + 22     &= 180  \\\\
a + 58          &= 180  \\\\
a               &= 180 - 58  \\\\
a               &= 122 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 122 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$122 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_angles_of_triangle

Let the size of the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 36 + 22     &= 180  \\\\
a + 58          &= 180  \\\\
a               &= 180 - 58  \\\\
a               &= 122 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 122 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$122 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B1CD7CC3-1C82-46B8-9F60-E03B0C68A78A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $4 \times (43.4 + 1.02)$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times (43.4 + 1.02)  \\\\
&= 4 \times 44.42 \\\\
&= 177.68
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$177.68$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times (43.4 + 1.02)  \\\\
&= 4 \times 44.42 \\\\
&= 177.68
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$177.68$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0FD0289F-6CF3-4995-B86D-7A270F63D72B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

To convert kilometers to miles I multiply by $5$ and then divide by $8$.

Using this method how many miles is $240 \ \text{kilometers}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using the given method,

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  240 \ \text {kilometers} \\\\
&=  (240 \times 5) \div 8 \ \text {miles} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {240 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {8 \times 30 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 30 \times 5} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&=  30 \times 5 \\\\
&=  150 \ \text {miles}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$150 \ \text {miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using the given method,

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  240 \ \text {kilometers} \\\\
&=  (240 \times 5) \div 8 \ \text {miles} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {240 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {8 \times 30 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 30 \times 5} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&=  30 \times 5 \\\\
&=  150 \ \text {miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$150 \ \text {miles}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3452FC7B-6354-498B-9BE2-BF8B78CD5FC9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of a day is $15 \ \text{minutes}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {1 \ \text{day}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {60 \times 24 \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {60 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {15 \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} } {\cancel {15} \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {4 \times 24}\\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {96}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {96}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {1 \ \text{day}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {60 \times 24 \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {60 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {15 \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} } {\cancel {15} \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {4 \times 24}\\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {96}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {96}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8AD4D8C4-1AB8-45CD-AF1F-11030CCEC600'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the missing number?

$4 \times 7 - 11 = 5 \times 2 + {?}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times 7 - 11     &= 5 \times 2 + a \\\\
28 - 11             &= 10 + a \\\\
a + 10              &= 17 \\\\
a                   &= 17 - 10 \\\\
a                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times 7 - 11     &= 5 \times 2 + a \\\\
28 - 11             &= 10 + a \\\\
a + 10              &= 17 \\\\
a                   &= 17 - 10 \\\\
a                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

How many seconds in an hour?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{hour} \\\\
&= 1 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 3600 \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3600 \ \text{seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{hour} \\\\
&= 1 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 3600 \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3600 \ \text{seconds}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E50AC62E-2A13-48E0-95A5-375ED2BF99DD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a bag there are $5$ red counters, $2$ blue counters, $7$ green counters and $1$ yellow counter.

What is the probability of picking a green counter?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Probability}  &= \dfrac { \text{ Favourable Outcomes} } { \text{Total Outcomes} }
 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {7} {5 + 2 + 7 + 1} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {7} {15}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {7} {15}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned} 
rtq_abbr_katex_formula_aligned_probability \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {7} {5 + 2 + 7 + 1} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {7} {15}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {7} {15}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-58963C70-4BF5-4BBC-9722-C4DB92F81077'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The school day starts at $08{:}55$ and finishes at $15{:}20$. How many minutes long is the school day?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{School duration}      &= 15{:}20 - 08{:}55 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{hour} \ 25 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 25  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 360 + 25 \\\\
                            &= 385  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$385  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{School duration}      &= 15{:}20 - 08{:}55 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{hour} \ 25 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 25  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 360 + 25 \\\\
                            &= 385  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$385  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B49A2D25-54CB-4ACB-A269-1B71CCD90AA4'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A medicine bottle contains $90$ pills. Each pill weighs $250 \  \text{mg}$, The full bottle weighs $160 \  \text{g}$. How much does the empty bottle weigh?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of pills}      &= 90 \times 250 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 90 \times \dfrac {250}{1000} \ \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {90 \times 250} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 25 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 25} {10} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {225} {10} \\\\
                                   &= 22.5 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty bottle}     &= 160 - 22.5 \\\\
                                   &= 137.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$137.5 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of pills}      &= 90 \times 250 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 90 \times \dfrac {250}{1000} \ \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {90 \times 250} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 25 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 25} {10} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {225} {10} \\\\
                                   &= 22.5 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty bottle}     &= 160 - 22.5 \\\\
                                   &= 137.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$137.5 \ \text {g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-14F5E25E-982B-4BD0-99BF-68170A5DA82C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What angle do the hours of a clock make between each other at $7{:}30$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Total angles for the complete round in a clock $= 360 \degree$.

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Angle between every hour}    &= \dfrac {360} {12} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {30 \times 12} {12} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel {12}} {12} \\\\
                                    &= 30 \degree
\end {aligned}
$

The angle the hours of a clock make between each other at $7{:}30$ is $30 \degree$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$30 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Total angles for the complete round in a clock $= 360 \degree$.

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Angle between every hour}    &= \dfrac {360} {12} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {30 \times 12} {12} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel {12}} {12} \\\\
                                    &= 30 \degree
\end {aligned}
$

The angle the hours of a clock make between each other at $7{:}30$ is $30 \degree$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$30 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-49429AE6-0143-4CCB-BB81-78DE7CA4E8F7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which fraction is $\dfrac {42} {63}$ equivalent to?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {42} {63} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 3 \times 2} {7 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times \cancel {3} \times 2} {\cancel {7} \times \cancel {3} \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2}{3} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {2}{3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {42} {63} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 3 \times 2} {7 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times \cancel {3} \times 2} {\cancel {7} \times \cancel {3} \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2}{3} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {2}{3}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-576FEBF3-4204-4EE6-B775-864A333384B2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a school $24\%$ of children have blond hair. If there are $1100$ children at a school, how many have blond hair?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that have blond hair}   &= 24 \% \ \text{of} \ 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24} {100} \times 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 1100} {100} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 11 \cancel {00} } {1 \cancel {00} } \\\\
                                       &= 24 \times 11 \\\\
                                       &= 264
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$264$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that have blond hair}   &= 24 \% \ \text{of} \ 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24} {100} \times 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 1100} {100} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 11 \cancel {00} } {1 \cancel {00} } \\\\
                                       &= 24 \times 11 \\\\
                                       &= 264
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$264$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B4B8928C-6D9A-4700-AA3D-DEEA64198181'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the square root of $225$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \sqrt[2]{225} \\\\
&= \sqrt[2]{5 \times 5 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
&= 5 \times 3 \\\\
&= 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3E93F536-692F-414B-82DB-9B94DEF5B8C2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What will be the $50 \text{th}$ number in the following sequence?

$1.23, 1.41, 1.59, 1.77, \ldots$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR:  Let's find the relation between the consecutive terms of the sequence.


The next term is calculated by adding $0.18$ to the previous term. Hence, the sequence would be:

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+0.18)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+0.18)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+0.18)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+0.18)} }  \\
1.23,  &&   1.41,  &&  1.59, &&  1.77, && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin {aligned}
50 \ \text {th number}  &= 1.23 + 49 \times 0.18 \\\\
                        &= 1.23 + 8.82 \\\\
                        &= 10.05
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10.05$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_sequence_find_difference_note

The next term is calculated by adding $0.18$ to the previous term. Hence, the sequence would be:

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(+0.18)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(+0.18)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(+0.18)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(+0.18)} }  \\
1.23,  &&   1.41,  &&  1.59, &&  1.77, && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin {aligned}
50 \ \text {th number}  &= 1.23 + 49 \times 0.18 \\\\
                        &= 1.23 + 8.82 \\\\
                        &= 10.05
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$10.05$
</code></pre>
</div>
